# Workflows 93: Implementation Review and Required Follow-ups

Status: active implementation record · Date: 2026-10-27

This is the canonical review backlog for P0–P5. A corrected specification is not evidence that its runtime consumer has been implemented. All required in-scope entries must close before the complete feature is declared done.

| ID | Priority / source | Finding and resolution | Remaining implementation gate |

## P1 task1.2 shared-session slice review

The independent read-only review found three cancellation/order races. All were fixed and the final recheck found no remaining concrete in-slice finding.

| ID | Priority / source | Finding and resolution | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W17 | P2; `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/session.rs:821` | A sent oneshot response was treated as delivered before its waiter consumed it. Offering now retains Invocation until a per-poll receipt; abandoned receipts recover queued ownership | Deterministic manual-poll test drops next() after send but before consumption, then receives the same event exactly once through a replacement poll |
| W18 | P2; `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/session.rs:782` | Biased selection did not recheck cancellation arriving between branch polls. Offer/receipt/completion and command dispatch now revalidate cancellation, owner closure and deadline before mutation | Direct transition tests cover cancelled/closed/expired attempts across offer, receipt and completion and require Closed with failed pending results |
| W19 | P2; `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/session.rs:768` | A receipt and following completion could arrive between select branch polls, leaving stale Offering/initialization state. Command dispatch now synchronously reconciles ready/closed receipts before matching commands | Ready-receipt→Complete succeeds; ready-receipt→InitError returns Busy and preserves the delivered attempt |

The actor owns an eight-command mailbox, one queued/offered/delivered attempt and one poll; per-attempt tickets prevent old cancellation from targeting new work. RPC envelopes carry their own deadline, readiness changes once, initialization notifies once, shutdown retains its join handle across cancelled waiters and caches failed settlement. Sixteen actor tests and the39 codec tests pass;23 runtime doctests and strict production-boundary Clippy pass. Workspace build/fmt/Clippy/rustdoc and1,822 tests pass (392 preexisting ignored). This review covers the shared actor only: the Native HTTP adapter still uses its existing session implementation until migration; the relay/socket client, Docker executor and ASL engine are not implemented by this slice. B01/B03 remain required.

Eleven real fixture tests cover slow/no polling, init failure, startup cancellation, version isolation, stale connections, busy cancellation and joined shutdown. ## P1 task1.2 Linux relay slice review

The independent read-only review found one documentation mismatch and two test-harness ownership gaps. A self-review also found a completed-worker error masking race; its fix was independently rechecked. The final review found no remaining concrete in-slice issue.

| ID | Priority / source | Finding and resolution | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W23 | P3; `specs/rustack-workflows-20-lambda-runtime-design.md` §3.4 | Retiring was described as Close-only while duplicate matching Cancel was deliberately idempotent. The contract now documents that exception without refreshing the lease, and distinguishes fatal session/attempt expiry from the bounded retirement handshake | Repeated busy/post-completion Cancel tests plus explicit real RIC cancellation qualification |
| W24 | P2; self-review, `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/relay.rs`, RelayActor::run | Priority stop could win while a completed IO error remained in JoinSet; teardown ignored that error. Drain now preserves completed IO failure/unexpected writer exit and ignores only joined abort cancellation or the expected queued-Close reader exit | Deterministic pre-completed EOF worker with priority stop requires EOF failure and a closed API listener |
| W25 | P2; `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/tests/docker.rs`, busy-marker loop | An outer timeout could cancel a CLI helper before explicit kill/reap. Forward one absolute deadline into the helper owner instead | Real docker wait helper expires and is reaped while the relay remains usable for Close/Ack |
| W26 | P2; same file, Environment::cleanup | A CLI reaping timeout returned before independent label reconciliation. Record cleanup errors, retain process ownership through reaping and still perform the label check before combining results | Every successful/failed setup, scope, pipe and handler case verifies no run-labeled container remains; final independent review confirms error aggregation order |

The relay binary uses safe nonblocking owned Linux pipe descriptors, task-bound tracing, bounded two-frame mailboxes and confirmed flush receipts. It separates relay Ready from first-RIC-poll BootstrapReady, retains borrowed attempt tickets across select cancellation, rejects invalid scope/order/direction, distinguishes runtime error payloads, and joins the shared API and control workers on every exit. Close and cancellation do not claim external function termination. Pinned provided.al2023 relay images are built by Make targets for both supported Linux architectures.

Workspace build/fmt/Clippy/rustdoc and1,853 tests pass. The ordinary suite has396 ignored tests:392 preexisting resource tests plus four explicitly gated Docker relay tests. Runtime:83 unit tests,39 doctests and strict production-boundary Clippy pass; the actual Linux entry point also passes cross-target Clippy. `make test-lambda-runtime-docker` explicitly ran all four tests with zero ignored on each arm64 VZ and amd64/QEMU daemon (eight executed test cases overall), proving official Python RIC computed42/7, warm counter1/2, request metadata, nonroot/read-only/cgroup limits, handler error, busy cancellation, Close/EOF/truncated/partial/idle exits, failed allocation and CLI helper timeout cleanup. Both daemons have zero remaining test-labeled containers. Native x86, Desktop and rootless qualification remain M4.

No dependency version/package was added; existing anyhow/serde_json are test dependencies and tracing-subscriber is also used by the relay entry point. Audit/deny report the same16 B01 advisories; bans/licenses/sources pass. B03 remains open. The production host control client, Docker deployment/pool/provider integration and ASL engine remain required; this is not full P1/M0 acceptance.

## P1 task1.2 host client / shared stream ownership review

The independent read-only review found two protocol-validation defects. Both were fixed and the final recheck found no remaining concrete in-slice finding. Self-review also corrected an error-class race exposed by the existing partial-frame regression after extracting stream ownership.

| ID | Priority / source | Finding and resolution | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W27 | P2; `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/client.rs`, ControlClient::next | Tokio timeout can poll an already-ready inner receive before its expired timer. Recheck the absolute clock after await and before frame/state mutation; shared send also rechecks after its flush receipt | Paused-clock pending waiter, capacity-one input and a queued valid frame reject after caller expiry; no false BootstrapReady |
| W28 | P3; same file, InitError handling | InitError was accepted after an earlier result proved delivery. Track proven delivery, including a discarded late cancellation result, and reject later initialization failures | Ordered Idle, later Busy and both cancellation-state cases fail closed after successful delivery |
| W29 | P2; self-review, `crates/rustack-lambda-runtime/src/transport.rs`, Transport::recv | Reader drops its sender before supervisor settlement, so an empty mailbox could report EOF before the actual parser/deadline failure. Wait for retained supervisor classification on channel closure | Existing paused partial-frame regression still requires Protocol/Deadline; completed EOF/priority-stop test preserves EOF and joined API closure |

The host validates ordered Hello/architecture/Ready and first-poll BootstrapReady, owns one active attempt, rejects foreign/duplicate/out-of-order events and discards at most one matching late outcome while cancelling. A write-in-progress flag prevents reuse after a dropped send future. Closing preserves validation of queued results/bootstrap/cancel acknowledgements; Ack/close cannot precede outstanding Cancel Ack. Cancelled next/shutdown waiters retain parsing and joined IO ownership. The same private transport supervisor now owns both relay and host stream workers, bounded mailboxes, terminal-frame preservation, flush receipts and cached failure settlement; the relay still separately joins its API before transport retirement.

Workspace build/test/fmt/Clippy/rustdoc pass with1,867 tests and397 ignored (392 preexisting plus five explicit Docker tests). Runtime:97 tests,47 doctests and strict production-boundary Clippy pass; the Linux entry point passes cross-target Clippy. All five resource-gated Docker tests explicitly pass with zero ignored on arm64 VZ and amd64/QEMU. The new case transfers actual CLI pipes into production ControlClient, performs computed42/7 and warm1/2, then tests separate real FunctionError and busy Cancel paths, joins streams, removes containers, reaps the CLI and reconciles its run label. This qualifies client/relay composition, not the future production Docker attach/deployment/pool/provider.

No dependency changes landed in this slice. The preceding audit remains unchanged B01 evidence; B01/B03 remain mandatory P1 gates. Docker startup/resource probes, production lifecycle integration and the ASL engine remain required by the complete P0–P5 objective.

## P1 task1.3 shared JSON preflight review

The independent read-only review found no concrete in-slice defect. It verified per-object decoded-key uniqueness, escaped keys, UTF-8 byte limits, encoded-string scratch bounds, aggregate member/element counting, root/container depth, malformed/trailing JSON, schema-error redaction and controlled key-limit configuration. The final recheck confirmed the generic parser does not hardcode Docker's256-byte key ceiling; consumers explicitly choose that tighter profile.

rustack-core::json now exposes validated nonzero JsonLimits, validate and from_slice<T>. A linear encoded-string scan precedes DeserializeSeed traversal; the traversal retains only bounded object key sets and builds no dynamic Value tree. Typed materialization follows successful preflight, and domain validation still belongs to the service. Core now forbids unsafe code. Four existing core documentation-format findings were corrected so its complete strict production-boundary check passes without ignores; B03's Lambda/auth/model debt remains open.

Core has39 passing tests (21 new JSON cases, including two512-case properties) and four passing doctests. `make test-json-boundaries` runs that suite and strict production Clippy. Workspace build/test/fmt/Clippy/rustdoc pass with1,888 tests and397 ignored resource tests. Existing proptest/rstest versions are reused only for core development; no dependency version/package was added. Audit/deny still report16 B01 advisories, with bans/licenses/sources passing. Docker metadata/stream consumers and all full P1/M0 exits remain required; a passing parser is not executor acceptance.

## P1 task1.3 bounded Docker wire review

The independent read-only review and final recheck found no remaining concrete in-slice issue after the lifecycle-wait correction below. Review covered typed operation paths and status handling, version negotiation, bounded serialization/JSON and HTTP framing, retained driver/handoff ownership, deadline/cancel races, worker panic settlement, strict multiplexed streaming and secret capture. Dedicated bounded32-job/eight-mailbox wait lane now leaves two slots for inspect/kill/remove/list; six short business slots remain separate | Held-two-waits regression completes Kill before either wait is released; business saturation admits Remove; exported attach survives wire shutdown without retaining a management slot. D20 records the ownership rule |

Each run independently removes both named containers, joins stream/wire owners and checks its run-label scope; subsequent daemon queries find no owned wire containers. These stress cases use60s IO/120s attempt budgets rather than claiming default5s performance. The separate five-case relay suite remains required regression evidence on each daemon.

It does not replace the stricter production framing decoder. Workspace/probe stacks align on Tokio1.54.1/Hyper1.12.0; root audit still has the same16 B01 advisories, deny bans/licenses/sources pass, and the standalone probe audit is clean. Deployment/image actors, remaining stream/network/probe consumers, warm pooling, startup/resource checks, full SDK/provider acceptance and B01/B03 remain required before P1 closes. ## P1 task1.3 owned image preparation review

The independent read-only design and implementation review verified typed private-only mutation endpoints, shared lease lifetime, retained cancellation/cleanup, digest/platform unpacked proof, and worker/root recovery. The final recheck found no remaining concrete in-slice issue. Source review preserves the HTTP500 uncertainty rule: a returned nested containerd gRPC error does not prove its server stopped committing.

| ID | Priority / source | Finding and resolution | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W39 | P2; runtime §2.9, private alias removal | Verify-ID and DELETE are separate operations; Docker has no expected-target CAS. Reserve the private namespace exclusively and state that concurrent external retargeting invalidates that guarantee. Observed mismatches quarantine without deletion | Wrong-target/repair regression; generated-only pin identities and force=false/noprune=true endpoints; pinned classic/containerd source |
| W40 | P3; `docker/lease.rs:65` | IO budget accepted subsecond positive durations despite the1..60s contract. Constructor now enforces the actual lower bound |1ns and over60s rejection plus typed config checks |
| W41 | P2; `docker/lease.rs:606`, self-review with independent recheck | Root failure with no registered rows could drop queued work without recording failure. Actor now records settled completion explicitly; abnormal Drop closes admission/marks failure even with zero rows | Empty-root failure, aborted-root cached failure, worker panic and off-runtime root Drop tests |
| W42 | P2; `docker/lease.rs:275`, self-review with independent recheck | Acquiring every semaphore permit to observe emptiness consumed other preparation capacity and distorted pin counts. Dedicated bounded count/watch observation now leaves admission capacity untouched | Wait-empty observer remains pending while a second legitimate preparation succeeds; clone lifetime and cancelled observer cases |

ImageLeaseManager now owns accepted preparations, live aliases and uncertain cleanup through retained workers/registry reservations. PinnedImage cannot be deserialized from metadata; clones keep the release channel alive, and shutdown revokes further use after consumers settle. ## P1 management IO and exact Docker resource review

D84 closes four ordinary SDK consuming scenarios through a transparent fixture-owned Unix proxy and real daemon: allocation acknowledgment loss during cancellation, held cleanup during cancellation, isolated image-inspection acknowledgment loss followed by full live transport loss, and two real Tag effects held across deletion/deployment saturation. The runtime fixes admission that could escape when a canceled deployment's outer observer completed while independent critical image mutation/retirement remained owned. It introduces no production injection hook, new dependency edge, lint waiver, advisory exception or daemon-wide shutdown.

|---|---|---|
| W257 | P3; independent proxy framing review, `tests/workflows/docker_io_proxy.py:35` | Reject terminated lines above 4096 before returning and check prospective complete encoded chunk/trailer limits before read/append. Controls cover exact encoded 20,477/+1 and terminated 4096/4097, in addition to ambiguous/truncated/oversized framing. |
| W258 | P2; actual fixture handoff observation, `tests/workflows/lambda_fault_sdk.py:10`, `tests/workflows/lambda_io_fault_sdk.py` | Guard the standalone entry PID print so importing shared helpers emits no extra handshake. A direct stdout-capture regression prevents parent server shutdown before SDK work. All startup/completion PID barriers stay independently bounded and exact-child checked. |
| W259 | P3; actual daemon protocol observation, `tests/workflows/docker_io_proxy.py:253` | A bound connection can legitimately carry identity GET/info followed by the operation GET/info. Forward both on the same backend connection; only the initial persistent identity response continues parsing. A control covers both image inspection and info as the second operation. |
| W260 | P2; ownership review prompted by actual capacity observation and controlled Tag regression, `crates/rustack-lambda-core/src/deployment/manager.rs:512`, `crates/rustack-lambda-core/src/docker/lease.rs:632` | Share accepted deployment admission into image preparation. Deletion settles the outer logical outcome without releasing the permit retained by critical image IO/retirement. A direct outer-worker join regression proves the row can disappear while capacity stays charged until actual cleanup. Existing cancellation/deadline/drop cases now distinguish logical settlement from physical admission release. |
| W261 | P2; independent unwind review, `crates/rustack-lambda-core/src/docker/lease.rs:564` Record | Retain preparation/deployment permits in the image actor record above the JoinSet and in emergency recovery, not only the worker stack. Existing worker/off-runtime-root panic controls now hold a rejected delete and prove both permits stay charged until positive physical recovery. | W262 | P2; independent handoff review, `crates/rustack-lambda-core/src/artifact/pinned.rs:148`, `crates/rustack-lambda-core/src/deployment.rs:492` | A shared private receipt releases admission only after ordinary prepare's validated consumed return or the exact successful image CAS. Unused inspection-barrier code was removed. |

## P5 immutable local image-lease reacquisition review (D87)

Task5.2a implements [runtime§2.15](./rustack-workflows-20-lambda-runtime-design.md#215-immutable-local-image-lease-reacquisition-d87), a prerequisite of atomic archive import. Independent source review before and after the controlled/actual-consumer additions finds no concrete correctness finding. This primitive does not remove provider/main archive rejection or complete5.2/P5; complete candidate validation, fresh lease/size checks and atomic startup publication remain5.2b.

`PreparationSource` makes ordinary reference preparation and saved metadata restoration separate variants; restore derives architecture from the validated image. The first operation uses only the canonical saved parent ID. A root mismatch rejects before a second platform lookup, then fresh selected-platform metadata must match the complete saved execution fingerprint before any journal/Tag effect. Missing local content never enters the pull branch or follows the saved source/registry references. A successful restore preserves the saved metadata and code hash while consuming fresh unpacked proof and the existing durable pin/retirement owner. Qualified architecture, absolute/independent IO budgets, quota, canceled-response and shutdown semantics are shared, not reimplemented.

Thirty-five lease controls pass (nine new/expanded cases). Positive source/immutable/platform request counters distinguish restore from ordinary fallback. An otherwise valid recomputed fingerprint with changed size/defaults/manifest/architecture rejects actual comparison and leaves no Tag, then correct restore recovers. A different initial root ID rejects before an extra platform request. Saved A remains A while its informational source pointsB; absent A404 never follows availableB. Existing canceled late Tag and bounded quota/timed-out shutdown controls now execute both source variants and retain real cleanup ownership. The qualified-platform rejection test also proves restored metadata cannot bypass platform admission. The first new test compile used the wrong existing error variant name; the final assertion uses DockerError::Image. The first all-target Clippy pass found a missing semicolon in a test match block; it was fixed without a waiver and the complete accepted profile reruns successfully.

Each retained engine passes18 actual resources: two existing production-wire/RIC resources now serialize/deserialize resolved metadata and consume restored relay/function leases before actual computation/override assertions, plus16 private-image/container recovery, namespace, reciprocal daemon binding, real approved pull, seven pool/environment and ordinary boto3 Executor/provider/HTTP cases. Explicit Make prerequisites rebuild selected relay/Python fixtures before capturing current source IDs. After every owner joins, complete all-state/image-prefix inventories show zero containers/runtime-private/test-source aliases/extra pull references and eight distinct captured post-provisioning source IDs preserved per engine. No new bridge is created, and both daemons/cache remain preserved.

|---|---|---|
| W281 | P2; self-review, `storage.rs::validated_zip_path` | Existing extraction treats every non-directory as a regular host file while rejecting only symlink/special-permission metadata. Reject FIFO/device/socket/other special-file declarations and file/directory type contradictions before staging or TAR conversion; share the checked validator and add hostile raw-attribute regressions. |
| W282 | P2; independent D89 contract review, runtime§6.2/Moby archive_tarcopyoptions_unix.go | copyUIDGID=true rewrites TAR ownership from Config.User and can invoke user-name resolution. Correct the spec before wire implementation: explicitly use false, preserve normalized TAR UID/GID0, and assert actual extracted ownership/modes. Bound retained Vec capacity as well as logical length. |
| W283 | P2; self-review, ZIP staging/converter tree bounds | Entry count alone permits millions of implicit directories from10,000 deep paths; implicit TAR parents also escape the promised directory mode and overhead budget. Before changing implementation, refine the shared profile to10,000 total explicit/implicit nodes, reject parent/file conflicts in either order and emit each normalized parent once. | W284 | P2; independent review, converter directory branch | Shared metadata checks reject declared size/CRC errors but skipping directory payloads never validates the compressed stream. Positively read one bounded byte and require EOF for empty directories in both consumers. | W285 | P3; actual fixture read-only oracle | A write-open of normalized0444/root-owned code can encounter DAC denial before a mount error. Remove an extra large administrative upload to the RO function, outside upload_code's RW-target precondition; independent review confirms no required evidence is lost. ## P5 retained producing code-volume owner review (D91)

**W289/P2 (spec defect, surfaced before correction):** caller-side polling/deadline alone cannot expire a queued Ready handoff when the prepare future remains live but unpolled. Runtime§6.7 now requires an independent owner timer and retained Pending/Consumed(Instant)/Expired receipt, timestamp reconciliation, revocation and physical cleanup before releasing an expired handoff's admission. Ready metadata cannot itself retain source/archive/resource permits after cleanup. Verify manual-poll queued abandonment/live-unpolled expiry/late-consumption, including on-time consumption processed after the timer.

**W290/P2:** independent initial source review found that retained staging still used observer-timed RawPort::request; its observation timeout could return before the accepted exchange/driver joined. Use the private owned-response bound-port view for every code stage and reaper RPC, preserving public observer semantics and post-join deadline fences. Verify delayed accepted upload/create responses and root loss retain source/admission/buffers before cleanup.

D91 consumer-contract refinement: add an accepted preparation handle and metadata-only retirement receipt before waiting for the result. A failed preparation result cannot itself authorize releasing a parent environment's memory/work reservation. Unaccepted envelopes publish a no-effect retirement proof when discarded; accepted records publish success only after physical and journal retirement. Expired receipt observation preserves the original cleanup owner. The convenience prepare API remains available for callers owning only code quotas.

**W291/P2:** independent review found that a queued retirement watch result could win `timeout_at` when repolled after its deadline. Add the post-observation deadline fence and manual-poll positive/closed-result regressions, preserving the same receipt for subsequent observation.

**W292/P2 (spec refinement surfaced before correction):** whole Tokio runtime loss can drop the transferred emergency ledger and release the producer semaphore before physical retirement. Add an acquisition-local sticky producer poison, armed by the exclusive guard and cleared only by an empty joined-ledger proof. An unclean final guard drop poisons the same acquisition; reconnecting under another runtime cannot restart it. Reacquisition remains subject to ordinary historical recovery. Verify two-runtime loss with an issued uncertain create and unchanged durable evidence, plus normal empty joined replacement.

**W293/P3:** final documentation review found the guide still described the frozen manifest as55 resources after D91 added the managed consumer. Correct the guide to56 and verify against the compiled exact inventory.

## P5 ordinary provided-runtime ZIP consumer preflight (D92)

Completed under the final qualification below. **W295/P2 (preflight refinement, surfaced before implementation):** joining a failed code stage without a receipt cannot by itself prove no accepted preparation. The consuming stage must publish its physical receipt before D91 can enqueue work. Add a private synchronous pre-enqueue publisher while a constructed no-effect retirement guard is held; publisher panic leaves unaccepted work or an already retained receipt. Test panic before/after publication, queue rejection and accepted stage loss. Keep a permanent per-plan allocation claim after actor commit so two managers cannot clean the same consumer.

**W296/P2 (spec correction surfaced before change):** D89 conversion admission waits on its bounded semaphore under the caller deadline, rather than always rejecting immediately with Busy. Preserve that contract; document admission expiry as ResourceNotReady and explicit Busy as throttling. Do not change the proven converter policy to satisfy a mistaken new spec sentence.

**W297/P2 (implementation review, fixed; final qualification below):** Create and CodeUpdate checked ZIP runtime capability before asynchronous fetch/conversion, allowing revocation during that wait to publish a successful deployment. Recheck the captured runtime/handler/architecture immediately before Create insertion and inside the final CodeUpdate revision CAS. Two held-S3-fetch controls revoke the executor after initial admission and require failure with absent Create or unchanged latest/published update state. Eight provider controls pass in `/tmp/rustack-d92-provider-sixth.log`; the earlier fifth invocation selected the wrong module filter and executed zero tests, so it is not evidence.

**W298/P2 (implementation review, fixed; final qualification below):** a cached producer shutdown error does not prove physical cleanup when the originating Tokio runtime destroyed its cleanup task. Retain an empty-ledger witness outside task destruction, publish it only after all entries and original workers joined (or a root dropped with both already empty), and require both that witness and observed task settlement before startup may advance to image retirement. Extend the two-runtime W292 regression to require false after loss and the joined root-failure regression to require true after positive empty cleanup. All46 owner controls pass in `/tmp/rustack-d92-owner-third.log`; failed test builds remain preserved separately.

## P5 managed ZIP archive preflight (D93)

The following preflight findings were recorded before production changes; final qualification remains in progress. **W306/P2 (independent spec preflight):** compressed-byte/revision caps do not bound retained extraction of repeated highly compressible code. **W307/P2 (raw-boundary spec preflight):** stripping ZIP bodies from an owned DTO cannot account for unknown CBOR fields that typed serde decoding already ignored. A128MiB service cap alone would let arbitrary fields borrow the enlarged ZIP allowance. Add a Lambda-specific schema-path policy to the existing raw CBOR scanner before typed allocation: only the exact latest/published version body paths can bypass the16MiB metadata charge, with bounded headers, keys, other scalars, nodes and depth. Keep generic codecs/limits and Native/Squib behavior unchanged; Image-only Docker uses zero body exemptions and its existing cap/schema; test misplaced keys/unknown fields with a Deserialize sentinel before any typed visitor runs.

**W308/P2 (original-deadline spec preflight):** current store restore calls each version's independent30s staging operation without the original import cutoff. Add an internal deadline-aware path and final pre-registration fence, preserving public Native/Squib behavior. Accepted blocking extraction retains only private source/TempDir/admission and no late store publication capability; caller expiry cannot be treated as worker settlement. ### Release packaging backlog for task5.4

**W371/P1 (independent out-of-phase release audit; open for5.4):** Dockerfile:10–13 copies workspace manifests/crates/apps/tests but omits patches/. **W373/P2 (same audit; open for5.4):** .github/workflows/release-docker.yml:28,63 gates publication only on build-rust, then publishes both architectures without executing those immutable images. Require same-commit exact-image qualification receipts before promotion; starting a default container/counting services is insufficient workflow/image acceptance. **W374/P3 (same audit; open for5.4):** Dockerfile:4 selects rust1.99.0 while rust-toolchain.toml selects1.99.1. **W375/P3 (same audit; open for5.4):** action.yml:131 sleeps0.5s while advancing ELAPSED by1, halving the advertised readiness timeout. Use an absolute deadline with validated input; cover the actual action readiness/failure paths.

**W378/P2 (independent D99 design reviews):** the initial Action design bounds readiness curl but gives Docker image lookup/pull/run no finite observer/termination/join contract. Specify one launch cutoff, separate finite per-command caps, one bounded direct subprocess owner, exact group termination/reaping and pre-effect nonsecret uncertainty retention; add real hung-child/pipe controls. Do not silently redefine wait-timeout to include image pulling.

**W379/P2 (same preflight):** the proposed region allowlist permits uppercase, while production settings.rs:928–934 rejects it. Narrow the Action boundary to lowercase [a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}, rejecting before Docker/runner output effects; do not widen production settings.

**W380/P2 (same preflight):** localhost HTTP200 alone does not establish readiness of the returned Action container. Require exact daemon/CID/source/nonce/port/running/healthy affiliation around a structured /_health/ready observation under one monotonic cutoff, unchanged StartedAt and meaningful wrong-publication/exited-CID/unrelated200/ready=false controls. All three design gaps are corrected before implementation; independent recheck remains required.


W378–W380 independently recheck clean before implementation; both design reviewers find no remaining concrete P0–P3 gap. Text checks validate18 files/613 relative links/322 fragments with zero errors and clean whitespace. Only the six design documents changed at this checkpoint; executable/source behavior remains unchanged and no D99 image/resource ran. Implementation and actual qualification remain required.


**W381/P2 (first actual Action process controls):** Darwin returns EPERM from killpg(group,0) while the terminated direct child remains an unreaped zombie. The first retirement loop aborts before wait, producing ResourceWarning rather than joined evidence. A controlled same-child probe confirms signal0 succeeds before TERM, returns EPERM before wait and ESRCH after wait. Reap that owned child and independently retry group observation; persistent denial stays failure, never absence. Preserve the original failed control log; fresh process controls and independent recheck remain required.

**W382/P2 (root Action source review):** curl --fail alone accepts3xx and reads ambient curl configuration, violating the exact local HTTP200 boundary. Put --disable first, disable proxies/other protocols, project the actual status separately and require200 without expanding the64KiB body budget; cover redirected status and argv policy.

**W383/P2 (independent Action implementation review):** emitting defined empty ACCESS_KEY/SECRET_KEY fails the installed settings boundary. Omit the absent pair/provider, retain empty caller exports, and reject empty credentials with enabled strict flags before effects. Skip-auth flags still govern; do not silently invent test credentials.

**W384/P2 (same review):** DOCKER_CONTEXT overrides DOCKER_HOST, but the initial client selected the host first. Resolve effective explicit context first, reject remote results even with a local host variable, then pin the local endpoint; add both-present controls.

**W385/P2 (same review):** runner output paths are opened write-only before regular-file checks, so a FIFO can block before handoff/cleanup. Add nonblocking/no-follow open and an actual FIFO control proving prompt failure and exact server cleanup.

**W386/P3 (same review):** a0.15s pre-spawn deadline can expire before a loaded child writes its PID marker, testing startup latency instead of retirement. Advance a controlled clock only after positive child entry, retain an independent real entry cutoff and assert actual PID absence.

**W387/P3 (same review):** lone-child/flood tests do not qualify the promised descendant-group boundary. Add a causally entered real parent/descendant, retain both PIDs and require both absent with settled pipes after forced observation.

**W388/P2 (same review):** the first Python control harness reused the new owner under test without its required independent Rust deadline/PID/capture boundary. Restore an explicitly selected exact Action process suite under the unchanged Rust Runner, with its own bounded manifest/listing; keep Native26/Docker76 counts separate. The normal YAML contract test remains independent of external resources. These implementation/control fixes require fresh component gates and independent recheck before closure.

**W390/P3 (actual D99 AMD prerequisite):** codes/tests.rs:71 assigns0xff to offset8 of a random owner UUID, producing a no-op whenever that byte is already0xff. **W391/P2 (actual exported root-image filesystem audit):** Dockerfile top-level COPY /runtime/tmp→/tmp and /runtime/data→/data do not preserve the new destination directory modes. **W393/P2 (independent artifact verifier review):** the prepared helper checked archive size only after docker image save joined. Captured image Size does not bound bytes emitted to disk. Install a helper-process POSIX RLIMIT_FSIZE hard/soft cap before CLI dispatch, preserving stricter inherited limits; every Runner child inherits it. Keep original child deadlines/retirement and post-join regular-file/link-count/byte checks. Add actual child output-limit proof.

**W394/P3 (same review):** truncated gzip raises EOFError outside the prepared verifier exception normalization. Catch it as the fixed ImageError diagnostic and add truncated-header/trailer and CRC controls; never expose archive-derived tracebacks.


**W395/P3 (artifact decompression recheck):** gzip also propagates zlib.error for a valid header with invalid DEFLATE data. Normalize that fixed failure alongside EOF/CRC errors and add a correctly rehashed blob/graph control with a reserved DEFLATE block. Kernel file-cap review otherwise rechecks clean.


**W396/P2 (second independent artifact verifier review):** normalized tarfile iteration consumes GNU longname/longlink/PAX internally, bypassing physical type/count checks and permitting recursive metadata chains before validation. Add a bounded raw512-byte header/checksum/type/path/size/count pass first, permit only one bounded local timing PAX per member, reject all GNU/global/chained metadata, and compare normalized member identities to the raw table. Add hidden/chained metadata negatives and an allowed timing-PAX positive. Dockerfile/Make wiring and other artifact bounds independently review clean.

## D101 direct Linux pure measurement review

The independent read-only review identified five valid in-scope findings; completion polish identified an additional Cargo record-count boundary, independently rechecked. All six are fixed; no findings remain in this dependency. The reviewer ran no tests or resource mutations.

| ID | Severity | Finding | Fix and evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W397 | P2 | The sum of per-group timeouts did not cover guest preflight/hash/report IO | Original guest450s/host530s POSIX alarms include local IO/publication; group deadlines clamp to the original cutoff, with independent late-preflight/alarm controls. |
| W398 | P2 | An original alarm could interrupt an already-active physical retirement | Share the retained Runner with the guard; defer signals only while Runner.retiring, preserve its original3s join allowance and fail afterward. An observed held child receives an alarm during positively observed retirement and must be absent as PID/group. |
| W399 | P2 | TERM/HUP/INT could bypass the Python owner's child retirement | Route all interruption handlers through the existing exception-driven owner and defer repeated signals during retirement; real-child controls signal each owner only after child entry and require failed owner plus PID/group absence. |
| W400 | P3 | Byte-bounded nested JSON could raise an uncaught recursion traceback | Reuse the existing depth8/member512/string16KiB metadata decoder, normalize malformed/recursive data and independently reject deep, oversized-member/string, duplicate and nonfinite inputs. |
| W401 | P3 | Short startup-relative test timers could race before held-child entry | Start the test phase clock only after observed child PID entry; deliver SIGALRM only during observed retirement and join the signal sender under the original test guard. |

| W402 | P3 | Cargo artifact streams had byte/per-document caps but no record-count admission | Reject over4096 records before decoding under the existing4MiB stream cap; literal4096-positive/4097-negative controls prove exact admission and no decoder entry on excess. |

The source implementation is [linux_performance.py](../tests/workflows/linux_performance.py), its independent controls are [test_linux_performance.py](../tests/workflows/test_linux_performance.py) and the executable contract is [verification§5.2](./rustack-workflows-70-verification-plan.md#52-direct-linux-arm64-pure-workflow-measurements-d101). Final gate/source/postcondition evidence is recorded in the [Linux measurement review](../docs/research/workflows-linux-performance-review.md). This prerequisite does not close dedicated-reference, image performance/load, Desktop/rootless, W373 or complete M4. D100's x86 waiver remains in force.

- **W403/P2:** active invocation counters alone cannot prove a readiness probe overlaps log flooding. ## D103 rootless bootstrap review W427–W434

Scope: [rootless bootstrap/attestation](./rustack-workflows-58-rootless-platform-design.md), not complete platform qualification. | Finding | Boundary and correction |
|---|---|
| W427/P2 | `rootless_platform.py` delegation: visible controllers alone did not prove delegation. Bind systemd DelegateControllers, manager enabled subtree and actual UID/writability of both delegated control files. |
| W428/P2 | Privileged system-file publication: check-then-install could overwrite a concurrent destination, and identical bytes alone did not prove safe metadata. Use root-owned same-directory fsynced temporary publication by no-replace link; require regular root-owned0644 exact bytes and safe parent metadata. The concurrent-publication control preserves unrelated bytes. |
| W429/P2 | Privileged lifetime: an unprivileged Runner cannot assume signalling authority over sudo descendants. Use uniquely recorded root systemd jobs with independent runtime/start/stop/cgroup kill bounds. An actual root TERM-ignoring process, group and exact service retire under those bounds. |
| W430/P2 | Pre-effect ownership: post-return receipts lost exact root-job identity on interruption. Fsync exact intent/argv/cutoff before dispatch and retain results separately; failed/lost replies keep the intent. |
| W431/P2 | Deadline reserve: intent IO could consume the privileged lifetime margin. Recheck lifetime plus full reserve after fsync; delayed publication must prevent dispatch without extending the cutoff. |
| W432/P2 | Observed existing-repository conflict: Colima already supplies Docker's signed source/key. Verify/reuse the exact canonical configuration/key and reject duplicate/unfamiliar sources before apt; preserve the original failed attempt. |
| W433/P2 | Observed early OS boundary: `/etc/os-release` is a symlink. Read the canonical trusted `/usr/lib/os-release`; allow explicit setup resumption only after proof that no guest setup state exists. |
| W434/P2 | Observed final report boundary: legitimate rootless sticky socket mode and subordinate socket group differ from a plain primary-group socket. Preserve safe sticky bits, record primary GID/maps, derive namespace socket GID991 and independently recover the completed installation proof read-only. |

No Rust code, dependency/toolchain/build/test target behavior changes in this dependency; Rust gates are not applicable. Required future rootless kernel/resource/functional/performance consumers, Desktop/reference/load/W373 and full M4 remain open. Keep `platformQualification=pending` until those exact consumers pass.

## PR76 CI regression corrections

- The Action smoke retained the pre-D113 predecessor sequence. Require the exact four-event sequence and IDs as before, with predecessors `[0,0,2,3]` per design10 and the original AWS observations. Keep all payload, state-name and pagination assertions.
- The native stale-revision test started an incomplete request before compressing and publishing the replacement ZIP, then incorrectly required at least four seconds of remaining cleanup. Publish the revision first, establish body collection using HTTP100 Continue, require replacement invocation to remain pending while the body is withheld, then release the request body before the independent drain deadline. Require the closed session response503 plus EOF and the old process to be reaped before accepting the replacement result. Withholding a body through the five-second graceful drain correctly fails settlement; the original success test must not require that hostile-peer path to succeed. No runtime deadline or production cleanup behavior changes.
- Stable rustfmt rejected a multiline format macro accepted by nightly. Express the same environment bytes through explicit concatenated literals and named format arguments. `make fmt-check` runs both formatters; the existing recorded Rust fmt gate consumes that target.

Code review verifies unchanged runtime behavior and request budgets, the exact D113 causal links, and removal of packaging time from the HTTP deadline observation. Validation results are recorded after execution; historical D114 receipts remain unchanged.
